THE CITY · Qwen 27B, government, 300 days, game 2
Everyone farms, everyone votes, a 10% tax fills a store nobody spends, and a mayor who proposes mandatory labour is voted out.
24 founders · 300 days · with a town hall · seed 2 · qwen
